<hr id=""answer"">

<ul><li>To engage in an activity with serious intent and determination, often without showing mercy. </li><li>Commitment to achieving a lasting outcome, rather than treating the endeavor as a casual or temporary effort.  </li><li>Origin : game of marbles, where ""playing for keeps"" meant that the winner would permanently keep the marbles he won, rather than returning them after the game.</li></ul>"

the canary in the coal mine	<ul><li>early warning sign or danger</li><li>canary = something fragile that reacts first -&gt; warns everyone else / signals a larger, hidden problem</li><li>coal miners carried canaries underground bc highly sensitive to toxic gases (CO, methane), if bird showed signs of distress or died, miners knew danger was imminent and evacuated</li></ul>
proxy	<ul><li>someone or something that&nbsp;<b>acts in place of another</b></li><li>from latin&nbsp;<i>procuratio / procurator</i>&nbsp;-&gt; one who manages or acts for another</li><li>proxy war (indirect actor to avoid direct involvement)</li><li>proxy vote (person authorized to act or vote for someone else, used in shareholder meetings)</li><li>proxy in tech (intermediary system between client and target) -&gt; web proxy, reverse proxy (protects backend servers)</li><li>everyday lang : proxy = substitute (surface behavior representing something deeper)</li><li>analytics : proxy = imperfect indicator for something that is hard to measure (GDP is a proxy for economic health)</li></ul>

Antiquarian	someone who studies, collects, or is interested in ancient objects, manuscripts, historical artifacts, and antiquities, usually from a scholarly or collector perspective
write-up	<div>technical report explaining how a problem was solved, usually step-by-step, including the reasoning, tools, and methodology used</div> <div></div>
fear porn	<ul><li>media content that deliberately plays on people's fears</li><li>mainly used online by the alt-right</li></ul>
-wise suffix	"<ul><li>""in terms of, with regard to""</li><li>Performance-wise, the app is solid -&gt; regarding of performance</li><li>Cost-wise, this is cheaper -&gt; in terms of cost</li><li>Clockwise -&gt; in the direction of a clock's movement</li><li>Crabwise -&gt; like a crab (sideways)</li><li>Bitwise -&gt; operating at the level of individual bits</li></ul>"
lewdness	behavior, language, or content that is sexually explicit, indecent, or offensive, especially in a way that violates social norms or laws
beltway	"<ul><li>highway that forms a loop around a city (ring road)</li><li>FR: boulevard périphérique</li><li>""inside the beltway"" -&gt; political world of Washington DC</li><ul><li>politicians, lobbyists, media, federal agencies</li><li>insider perspective, disconnection from everyday citizens</li></ul></ul>"
black-bag job	<ul><li>covert, illegal entry into a location to conduct intelligence or investigate</li><li>ex. Watergate scandal</li><li>black bag: burglars/spies would carry tools in a black bag -&gt; lockpicks, skeleton keys, etc.</li></ul>
tamper	<ul><li>alter / manipulate something improperly, in a way that can damage or falsify it</li><li>tamper with evidence: change/interfere with evidence</li><li>data tampering: unauthorized modification of data</li><li>tamper-proof seal: shows if someone opened of altered it</li></ul>
